Sam Darnold’s cratering play is a big picture problem for the Seahawks

📅 Nov 03, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

Seattle Seahawks quarterback Sam Darnold is a Super Bowl champion who played an important part in bringing another Lombardi Trophy to Seattle, and that can never be taken away.

Sam Darnold is also playing bad football.

You can blame the offensive coordinator, you can blame the offensive line, you can blame the lack of a running game, but Sam Darnold is playing bottom-tier football. His worst throw from Monday night's blowout loss against the Chicago Bears didn't even count, but it's emblematic of his incomprehensible decision-making.

Incidentally, the play design is pretty much DOA but there is no need to turn 2nd and 1 into a double coverage gamble.

Darnold's touchdown-to-interception ratio is barely 1:1 over his last 16 regular season starts. His mostly great playoff run is the break-up in what is otherwise a prolonged stretch of play that is far removed from much of his 2024 Minnesota Vikings season and the first 10-ish games of his Seahawks career.

(Editor's note: It's 344/551 and not 334)

There are no advanced stats that can positively spin Darnold as an above-average starter at the moment. He really wasn't playing particularly well for most of the back-end of 2025, but the running game finally sprung to life and Seattle's defense and special teams repeatedly either scored or gave the Seahawks extremely short fields.

The 2026 Seahawks are 4-4, which is not the end of the world, but how they've gotten to 4-4 is pretty much the worst case scenario in the short- and long-term. If Mike Macdonald's defense hypothetically had curiously dropped off to be a middling group and the Seahawks were losing shootouts while Darnold was putting up impressive numbers, I think we could live with that and acknowledge that defenses can be more volatile year-to-year than offenses. Darnold is heavily culpable for at least three of the four defeats, and even his best game against the Washington Commanders is significantly impacted by what was effectively a game-losing pick-six.

Darnold's contract is a great mid-level contract for someone with his track record and roughly two seasons worth of good productivity. It's also a contract that expires in 2027, and the current version of Darnold does not look like someone who should be Seattle's Week 1 starter, much less the starter in 2028.

Barring substantial improvement over the second half of the season, John Schneider has a major decision to make on Darnold, regardless of whether or not Brian Fleury is still the offensive coordinator. We've seen Darnold be very good when paired with the right play-caller, and I am not going to put the failures of this offense exclusively at his feet. But Darnold is in that tier of quarterback who needs way more help to be functional than the likes of Josh Allen or Joe Burrow or Patrick Mahomes. Every quarterback needs at least some semblance of foundational support to succeed, but Sam needs way more stuff than, say, prime Russell Wilson. That includes, seemingly above all else, having an experienced and effective play-caller who can play to his strengths and minimize his worst habits. Brian Fleury does not seem to fit the bill.

Darnold also needs play-action to work like we need air to breathe. When it's not working, Darnold has consistently been anywhere from average to ruinous in pure dropback situations. He needs an offensive line to protect him so well that his pressure rate is low, otherwise this is what you get.

Darnold with a clean pocket and/or the ability to get the ball out of his hands quickly can sling it with the best of them. When he is dealing with pressure in his face, he is barely a starting caliber quarterback. We have two years of evidence in a Seahawks uniform.

Play-action to be so insanely efficient that it overrides the lack of non-PA success.

Favorable game scripts such that his team is almost never trailing.

Related: A top-5 caliber defense such that his team is rarely trailing.

An offensive coordinator/play-caller who can scheme up easy-button throws for fun.

An offensive line that can pass protect competently.

An explosive running game in case none of the above works out or is figured out by NFL defenses.

There's nothing wrong with building a great environment around a competent but limited quarterback, but that also means a crevice turns into the Grand Canyon when said environment is even marginally worse. You can live with Darnold's turnovers if he's as efficient as he was in 2024 and 2025. That is not even close to what we're seeing in 2026.

The possibility also cannot be discounted that two years of film effectively exhausted all of the routes that the likes of Kevin O'Connell and Klint Kubiak could make Darnold thrive. As I wrote not too long ago, "With non-elite quarterbacks there are only so many levers you can pull before competent defenses respond." If Seattle sticks with Sam then it will likely need an(other) offensive coordinator to get him back on track, all while not being an immediate head coach candidate. This is the downside of having a defensive minded coach and your OC hires are either bad (see: Grubb, Ryan) or are so good that NFL teams take notice (see: Kubiak, Klint). The churn truly never stops unless you find that unicorn who can be a stable part of your organization while being no threat to hold a head coaching job.

Seattle's roster as a whole is a very good one; most of the players from last year's Super Bowl squad are still here. Any premise of Seattle having a sustained window of playoff and Super Bowl contention was contingent upon Darnold sustaining his success and mercifully giving us all a break from debating Seattle's next franchise quarterback. If Darnold cannot recapture even half of what made him go from draft bust to unlikely Super Bowl champion—this includes the rest of this year and (if applicable) his contract year in 2027—the Seahawks' long-term vision is suddenly a lot more complicated. Drew Lock is a free agent and frankly I don't want to bank on the QB revival thing yet again for a career backup entering Year 9. It's not like Drew himself hasn't had a history of being turnover prone with volatile week-to-week performances when he was a starter elsewhere. Jalen Milroe is a great unknown outside of preseason, and 2027 is a pivotal year for him to see if he's even ready to be an NFL starter. Other than that? Seattle might find itself once again dealing with major uncertainty at the most important position in the sport.

That's what stings the most, even coming off an unforgettable Super Bowl season. The Seahawks have their head coach, have a lot of hyper-talented, young core pieces under contract either on rookie deals or massive extensions, but quarterback has become a giant question mark now and therefore makes for a much murkier future than hoped.

What's New At Costco: November 2026 Edition

📅 Nov 03, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

We may receive a commission on purchases made from links.

November has arrived, and while it's still fall and the time to enjoy the flavors of the season, retailers and consumers alike are gearing up for the holidays, beginning with Thanksgiving and continuing on to Christmas, Hanukkah, and other celebrations. As such, shelves at your favorite stores are being cleared of Halloween decorations and bags of trick-or-treat candy in favor of cornucopia motifs, pumpkin pies (some of which we've ranked), and turkeys. You may also begin to see Christmas trees, ornaments, and gift sets appear to inspire customers' holiday gift-giving.

Like every month, Costco has announced new items that it's carrying in stores and online. The popular warehouse club is known for offering great value on bulk items, including multipacks of many popular brands. It also has many exclusive items that you may be hard-pressed to find at other stores, and you can now find an ever-increasing array of international products, too, along with items from its in-house Kirkland Signature brand. Below are our picks for some of the most noteworthy items available in November. They include some holiday-ready essentials and beautiful gifts for loved ones and yourself, including a decadent dessert for Thanksgiving. You'll also find some everyday essentials. Just keep in mind that availability and prices may vary by location.

Toblerone Biscoff Milk Chocolate Truffles with Caramelized Biscuit Filling are available at Costco for $11.99.

Caramel apples are a favorite seasonal treat typically associated with fall, and the Mrs. Prindables brand has added a festive touch to these confections that makes them perfect for the winter holidays. Each one is covered in caramel and then dipped in a layer of chocolate, and you get a mix of triple chocolate and chocolate and walnut varieties in each pack. They're individually wrapped and include holiday stickers and decorative bows, making them perfect for gifting.

Mrs. Prindables Happy Holidays Caramel Apples are available at Costco in a six-pack for $59.99.

Wild boar was once something that you could only find at gourmet markets or through mail-order companies, but it's become more common at supermarkets, and you can now get a whole shoulder of it at Costco. Imagine pork but with a richer flavor and a hint of nuttiness, and you can understand why it's prized by many chefs. You can use this cut to make one impressive centerpiece for a holiday spread or divide it into smaller portions to use in different dishes, including a variety of styles of chili.

Northfork Wild Boar Boneless Shoulder is available at Costco in a 5-pound package for $119.99.

Olipop is one of Food Republic's top-ranking prebiotic sodas that also has less sugar than traditional options. This limited-time winter variety pack includes cola, ginger ale, and a new pomegranate flavor. They also all feature a cute yeti on the can, along with some seasonal graphics that remind you that the holidays are just around the corner.

Olipop Soda, Winter Variety Pack is available at Costco for $23.99.

This gift box is perfect for those who love Swedish-style gummies and features 12 2-ounce containers, each holding a different type of chewy treat. They're all arranged in an attractive gold and white box, and flavors include everything from mouth-puckering sour varieties to creamy ones that have a pleasant, fluffy texture on top of the chew. They even include fun shapes like lips, eyes, and pacifiers.

Damn Man Crave Collection Gift Box is available at Costco for $49.99.

S'mores are a favorite treat for a reason: The combination of gooey toasted marshmallows, chocolate, and graham crackers just hits in a way few other snacks can. The only drawback is that they can take some effort to make, which is why these ready-made s'mores clusters are so deliciously convenient. Not only do they feature the traditional trifecta of ingredients, but they also include kettle-cooked caramel for extra gooeyness.

Kirkland Signature Caramel S'mores Clusters are available at Costco in a two-pack for $34.99.

While matcha drinks and foods are widely available now, few things beat making it yourself, especially if you love the ritualistic process. This gift set includes everything needed to get you or a lucky recipient started on a matcha journey, including a beautiful ceramic bowl and a bamboo whisk (rather than a metal one). In addition to a tin of ceremonial-grade organic matcha, this set also includes vanilla matcha that's perfect for making lattes.

Whittard Matcha Experience Gift Box is available at Costco for $89.99.

You typically need to add other ingredients to a pasta dish in order to get certain nutritional benefits, but that's not the case with this protein pasta from the Al Dente brand. Each serving includes 30 grams of protein and 14 grams of fiber, and it contains 40% fewer carbohydrates than conventional pasta. The variety pack comes with spiraled fusilli and ridged elbows that are perfect for making the best macaroni and cheese.

The Al Dente Fusilli and Elbows Protein Pasta Variety Pack is available at Costco in a six-pack for $29.99.

A snack bar is a great way to satisfy a sweet craving between meals without quite feeling like a dessert or candy. There are many brands on the market, and if you can't decide on just one, then you'll enjoy this variety bundle, which features six popular brands of snack bars, including the warehouse club's in-house Kirkland Signature brand. The assortment includes crunchy and nutty bars, soft ones with fruit fillings, and more, so you'll never get bored.

The Snack Bar Variety Bundle is available at Costco for $39.99.

You may see the words "colossal cashews jar" and think that this Costco item refers to the size of the container. While the attractive 30-ounce glass jar does hold a lot of nuts, the cashews themselves are what give this product its plus-sized name. They are large, roasted to a perfect golden color, and seasoned simply with a touch of salt.

A.L. Schutzman Colossal Cashews Jar is available at Costco for $36.99.

November is peak fall season, meaning that pumpkin is the flavor of the month, including at Costco. This 8-inch cheesecake from Ferrara's Bakery comes topped with rich caramel and pecans, giving you the best of both pecan pie and pumpkin pie (which Costco is already known for) in cheesecake form. There are two to a pack, meaning you can bring one to a party and keep one in the fridge just for yourself.

You can purchase a two-pack of Ferrara's Bakery Pumpkin Cheesecake with Caramel and Pecan Topping at Costco for $119.99.

Hyundai’s electric SUV finds some footing, with IONIQ 5 sales topping 33,000 in 2026

📅 Nov 03, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

The Hyundai IONIQ 5 remains one of America’s most popular EVs outside Tesla, with October sales up 21%.

While the Tesla Model Y and Model 3 still dominate the US electric vehicle market, accounting for about 50% of all sales through the third quarter, it was the Hyundai IONIQ 5 that ranked third.

The market has slowed since the $7,500 federal tax credit was removed last September, with several automakers reporting fewer EV sales than in 2025 when buyers rushed to claim the credit.

Hyundai responded by cutting prices on the 2026 IONIQ 5 by up to nearly $10,000 on some trims, with a new starting MSRP of around $35,000.

As the market shows signs of recovery, IONIQ 5 sales bounced back in October, rising 21% to 1,981 units.

Through the first 10 months of 2026, Hyundai sold 33,093 IONIQ 5s in the US, still down 23% from the more than 42,700 it sold during the same period last year, when credit was still available.

Hyundai said the IONIQ 5 helped drive growth as the company posted its best October in the US ever with 73,452 vehicles sold, a 5% increase YOY.

While Hyundai sold only 169 IONIQ 9s last month (-47%), sales are still up 49% through October, with nearly 6,700 units sold.

Hyundai’s hybrids set a new October sales record, rising 20% from last year and accounting for 29% of total sales in October. Through October, Hyundai’s total US sales are up 3% YOY at 770,916 units.

Since not all automakers report monthly sales figures, it’s hard to determine where the IONIQ 5 stands through October.

Earlier today, Hyundai’s sister brand Kia reported that sales of its three-row electric SUV, the EV9, were up 11% year over year in October. EV9 sales are now up 5% year-to-date, with 13,835 units sold through October.

The cheapest 2027 Hyundai IONIQ 5 SE Standard Range starts at $35,250 and offers up to 245 miles of range.

If you want more range, upgrading to the extended-range SE RWD model costs an extra $2,500, but you get up to 318 miles of range on a single charge.

Impact: Expect Sam Roush to serve as the team's TE2 for the remainder of the season, while second-year tight end Colston Loveland should continue his recent ascension back into TE1 territory.

Impact: Fantasy managers will want to monitor his situation in the coming week, although Watson appears to have avoided significant injury.

Packers head coach Matt LaFleur said on Tuesday that it is "way too early" to determine whether Reed will play again in 2026. That said, he was not placed on injured reserve ahead of their Week 3 contest. He will reportedly undergo surgery.

Impact: Should he miss time, the Packers' star players would further consolidate touches.

Dan James has surely just saved his Leeds career - verdict

📅 Nov 03, 2026  |  Category: Business  |  Read Source →

Senior Correspondent AUTHORITY Senior football journalist specialising in refereeing and officiating; former contributor to The Football League Paper, Late Tackle, and the Premier League; University of Derby graduate. FOCUS Refereeing controversies, football finance and governance, PGMOL decisions, and officiating analysis across the Premier League, EFL, and SPFL. THE INSIGHT Harri utilises a network of officiating and industry contacts to deliver verified, expert analysis of refereeing decisions and PGMOL policy. He provides the regulatory clarity behind the controversy to ensure fans get the full picture.

Dan James' future at Elland Road has been a huge talking point amongst Leeds United fans so far this season.

Of course, Daniel Farke's contract situation has been the most-discussed top, and there has yet to be any movement on that situation.

Despite his injury problems, James remains one of the manager's favourite players, even with continuous links to a move away.

Football Insider revealed last month that the Welshman could push for an exit in the January window, but Farke is unlikely to want to that to happen.

The manager showed his faith in his player for the Whites' away clash against Bournemouth on Saturday, 31 October.

Sadly, it ended in defeat, with goals from Evanilson and Amine Adli, meaning that Leeds are now without a win in five straight matches.

If James can reproduce his performances in upcoming games, though, Leeds might be able to turn that form around after the international break.

He may have only lasted 65 minutes before being replaced, but it was a great cameo for the 28-year-old at the Vitality Stadium.

James reached the joint-highest speed in the game, completed 100 per cent of his dribbles, the most of anyone, and made six line-breaking passes [FotMob].

Defensively, he was just as important, making four recoveries and two clearances, as well as diving into seven duels and committing two fouls.

It was exactly the kind of performance that Leeds fans will have wanted to see from the rarely-seen winger, especially with Noah Okafor struggling.

The Whites will be desperate to get back to winning ways against Tottenham Hotspur on Saturday, 7 November, especially at home.

From the Bournemouth game, there are several players who could be forced to sit out based on their performances.

James Trafford made mistakes, but there's no doubt that he'll bounce back. The same can't be said for Ao Tanaka, Gabriel Gudmundsson or Justin Bogle.

Brenden Aaronson, Lukas Nmecha and Sean Longstaff all offered very little off the bench, so maybe Farke doesn't have many changes that he can make.
